To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Tight Algorithm and Hardness for Submodular Linear Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20202)
*Evan Abboud,Roy Schwartz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了子模函数下最小线性排序问题的近似难度，证明了其最优近似比为 $\Theta(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$，并提供了一个匹配该下界的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小线性排序问题在地函数下的表现已知，但在子模（submodular）函数的一般情形下，其近似难度尚未得到完全解决。为了解决这一差距，研究者旨在为该问题确定紧致的上下界。

Method: 开发了一种新的多项式时间算法，实现了 $O(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$ 近似比；同时，基于信息论导出了相应的下界结论，证明了任何在多项式时间内评估 $f$ 的算法都无法达到 $o(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$ 的近似比。

Result: 给出了 $O(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$ 的近似算法，并证明了相应的信息论硬度下界（$o(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$ 不可近似），完善了该领域对子模函数下最小线性排序问题的研究。

Conclusion: 确定了最小线性排序问题（Minimum Linear Ordering Problem）在子模函数下的最优近似比为 $\Theta(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$。该结果通过理论下界匹配了一个新的多项式时间算法，从而解决了该问题的近似难度问题。

Abstract: We consider the Minimum Linear Ordering Problem: given a ground set $N$ of cardinality $n$ and a non-negative set function $f\colon 2^N\rightarrow \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}$, the goal is to find an ordering $π$ of $N$ that minimizes the sum of the values of $f$ over all prefixes of $π$. This problem has been studied for various classes of set functions, and the case of a submodular $f$ is of special interest, as it captures classic problems including Minimum Linear Arrangement and Minimum Containing Interval Graph. In this work, we resolve the approximability of the Minimum Linear Ordering Problem for a general submodular $f$ by establishing matching upper and lower bounds and present: $(1)$ a polynomial-time algorithm achieving an $O(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$-approximation; and $(2)$ a matching information-theoretic hardness result, showing that no algorithm evaluating $f$ a polynomial number of times can achieve an $o(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$-approximation. Previously, the best known hardness of approximation was $2$, and an $O(\sqrt{n/\ln n})$-approximation was known only for the special case where $f$ is both submodular and symmetric.

</details>


### [253] [Computing Twin-Width via Treedepth and Vertex Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20331)
*Robert Ganian,Mathis Rocton*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入树深与顶点完整度作为参数，解决了双宽（twin-width）计算中的长期算法瓶颈，证明了在这些参数下双宽的近似及精确计算均属固定参数易解（FPT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双宽作为图论中的核心参数，在解释一阶模型检测的固定参数易解性方面具有重要地位，但目前计算双宽的难度较大（寻找最优收缩序列是 NP-hard），且缺乏以双宽本身为参数的有效近似算法。因此，亟需寻找其他参数化途径来突破计算瓶颈。

Method: 1. 利用树深（treedepth）作为参数，实现了双宽的固定参数近似算法，证明了定向双宽（oriented twin-width）在算法处理上的优势。
2. 利用顶点完整度（vertex integrity）作为参数，设计了第一个用于精确计算双宽优化收缩序列的非平凡固定参数算法。

Result: 1. 证明了在树深参数化下，双宽的近似计算是固定参数易解的，突破了以往仅限于删除距离（deletion distance）参数化的限制。
2. 证明了在顶点完整度参数化下，双宽的精确计算是固定参数易解的，这是首个关于最优收缩序列的非平凡算法结果。

Conclusion: 研究通过利用树深（treedepth）和顶点完整度（vertex integrity）这两个参数，突破了双宽（twin-width）计算的复杂性壁垒，证明了在这些参数下，双宽的近似和精确计算是固定参数易解（FPT）的。

Abstract: Twin-width is a graph parameter that has become central to explaining the fixed-parameter tractability of first-order model checking across many graph classes. Despite its algorithmic importance, computing twin-width remains poorly understood: even recognizing graphs of twin-width at most four is NP-hard, and no fixed-parameter approximations parameterized by twin-width itself are known. A recent approach towards breaking this barrier focuses on first developing fixed-parameter algorithms for computing or approximating twin-width under parameterizations distinct from twin-width.
  Our first result establishes that approximating twin-width is fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized by treedepth, thereby breaking the long-standing barrier that all previous tractable parameterizations were based on deletion distance. The proof proceeds via oriented twin-width, yielding the first constructive evidence that this variant may be easier to handle algorithmically. As our second main result, we show that computing twin-width exactly is fixed-parameter tractable with respect to vertex integrity. This constitutes the first non-trivial parameterized algorithm for computing optimal contraction sequences.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [REMOP: REmote-Memory-aware OPerator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19576)
*Shiquan Zhang,Yunhao Mao,Yuqiu Zhang,Gengrui Zhang,Jeyhun Karimov,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: REMOP是一个针对远端内存环境的查询算子优化框架，通过在开销模型中考虑传输轮次而非仅计算I/O总量，显著提升了内存受限下的数据库溢出性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远端内存架构下，数据溢出（spilling）的性能开销不仅取决于I/O总量，还受限于数据传输的往返延迟（round-trip latency）。传统的以“最小化I/O总量”为目标的磁盘溢出策略在远端内存中会导致过多的传输轮次，进而导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了REMOP（Remote-memory-aware operator optimization）框架。该方法在延迟成本模型中引入了“传输轮次（transfer rounds）”这一维度，并针对阻塞嵌套循环连接（BNLJ）、外部归并排序和外部哈希连接推导出了特定的缓冲区划分策略。

Result: 在双节点计算-内存测试平台上的评估显示，REMOP在溢出密集型基准测试中将传输轮次降低了97%，算子运行时延降低了48%；在TPC-H和TPC-DS查询中，平均端到端运行时延分别降低了22.7%和26.4%。

Conclusion: REMOP通过将传输轮次（transfer rounds）纳入开销模型，有效优化了远端内存环境下的查询算子性能，显著降低了运行时延和端到端查询时间，证明了远端内存环境下算子策略需从最小化I/O总量转向最小化传输轮次。

Abstract: Remote and disaggregated memory tiers expand the effective memory capacity of analytical database engines, but they also reshape the cost structure of out-of-memory query processing. When an operator spills beyond local DRAM, moving pages to remote memory incurs both data-transfer time and a fixed round-trip latency per transfer. Classical operator analyses and buffer-allocation heuristics primarily target disk spilling by minimizing total I/O volume. Under remote memory, these strategies can be suboptimal because they may trigger excessive transfer rounds. We present REMOP, a remote-memory-aware operator optimization framework that uses transfer-round-aware intra-operator memory policies to improve out-of-memory execution under tight memory budgets. REMOP introduces the number of transfer rounds into the latency cost model and derives operator-specific buffer-partitioning strategies, instantiating the approach for blocked nested-loop join, external merge sort, and external hash join in DuckDB. Our evaluation on a two-node compute-memory testbed shows that REMOP reduces transfer rounds by up to 97% and operator runtime by up to 48% on spill-heavy microbenchmarks, and lowers the average runtime of spilling TPC-H and TPC-DS queries by 22.7% and 26.4% end-to-end.

</details>


### [255] [DeQL: A Decision Query Language for Prescriptive Analytics over Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19751)
*Matteo Brucato,Fjodor Kholodkov,Soren Little,Jakob Mayer,Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DeQL 是一种 SQL 扩展语言，它引入 `CREATE CANDIDATES` 和 `DECIDE` 语法，让用户能够通过声明式的方式直接在数据库内进行决策优化，将“最优方案求解”过程集成到 SQL 的关系处理逻辑中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据处理流程中，从关系型数据库提取数据、建模并求解最优决策方案的过程高度离散且复杂，缺乏一种统一的、声明式的语言来直接在数据库内部描述和求解决策优化问题。

Method: 提出了两种核心 SQL 扩展语法：CREATE CANDIDATES（定义选项候选空间）和 DECIDE（定义决策变量、约束条件及优化目标）。引擎利用这些声明性结构自动解析并将问题转化为底层的优化求解。

Result: 成功构建了 DeQL 语言规范，不仅支持基础的子集选择、分配、调度等优化任务，还扩展支持了不确定性模型、内联模型评分以及性能限界求解，并通过原型验证了其在决策优化场景下的可行性。

Conclusion: DeQL 是一种能够以声明式方式将决策优化问题集成进标准 SQL 的新型查询语言，简化了在关系型数据上进行复杂优化决策的开发难度，并保持了 SQL 的声明式和关系代数属性。

Abstract: DeQL (Decision Query Language) extends SQL to express decision queries: given options drawn from relational data, constraints from policy, and a measurable objective, a DeQL query computes the best course of action. Two constructs carry the extension: CREATE CANDIDATES, which defines the space of options from relational sources, and DECIDE, which declares decision variables, named constraints, and an objective over them. The design follows SQL's principles: the user states what to optimize while the engine chooses how to solve it, every query consumes and produces relations, and the structure of a problem stays visible to the engine. This document specifies the language (its design principles, syntax, formal grammar, and execution model) with examples spanning subset selection, allocation, assignment, scheduling, and decisions at multiple levels of aggregation, and extensions for optimization under uncertainty, inline model scoring, and time- and quality-bounded solving. It is the first version of the specification; the language is under active development, and this version fixes the core constructs on which later revisions will build.

</details>


### [256] [Policy-aware Vector Search: A Vision for Fine Grained Access Control in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19803)
*Lakshmi Sahithi Yalamarthi,Primal Pappachan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨了矢量数据库在细粒度访问控制（FGAC）方面的安全短板，提出了“策略感知矢量搜索”的概念模型，并分析了在保障数据安全的同时维持高效语义检索的路径与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RAG和AI管道在安全敏感领域的普及，矢量数据库普遍缺乏对细粒度访问控制（FGAC）的支持，而在处理非结构化语义检索时，如何在保证访问合规性的同时维持高检索效率成为核心痛点。

Method: 通过形式化定义矢量数据库中的FGAC策略模型及其执行问题，对比分析了多种FGAC实施策略，并开展了初步的性能评估与实验探索。

Result: 梳理了矢量数据库实现FGAC的内在矛盾（即安全性与高性能ANN搜索的冲突），评估了现有策略的执行效果，并明确了未来实现策略感知矢量搜索（Policy-aware Vector Search）的关键技术挑战。

Conclusion: 矢量数据库在实现细粒度访问控制（FGAC）方面面临严峻挑战，目前迫切需要制定标准化的政策模型和高效的执行策略，以在权限合规、搜索召回率与查询延迟之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly used in security sensitive contexts with Retrieval Augmented Generation and organizational AI pipelines; however, their security capabilities remain limited. Specifically, Fine-grained Access Control (FGAC) which is required to ensure that data access adheres to user-specific policies is not fully supported in modern vector databases. Unlike relational databases, vector databases combine structured and unstructured attributes to provide semantic, approximate query results, which complicates FGAC implementation. This creates an inherent tension between enforcing FGAC policies correctly, achieving high ANN search recall and maintaining low query latency. In this paper, we present a vision for Policy-aware Vector Search by formalizing the FGAC policy model in vector databases as well as the enforcement problem. We compare various enforcement strategies, present preliminary findings, and identify key open challenges for future research in policy-aware vector search.

</details>


### [257] [Query-aware Routing for Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19898)
*Qianqian Xiong,Mengxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对过滤后向量搜索（Filtered ANN）中不存在单一最优算法的问题，本文提出了一种基于轻量级模型和离线基准表的查询感知路由框架，能够动态选择最优算法，显著提升了检索的召回率与性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过滤ANN（Filtered ANN）方法在不同的数据集和谓词下表现各异，不存在一个通用的“最优”算法，甚至同一场景下的最佳选择也会随查询而变。因此需要一种机制在查询时刻动态选择最合适的算法。

Method: 提出一种查询感知路由框架（Query-Aware Routing Framework）：利用轻量级机器学习模型预测候选项的召回率，结合离线基准表，在保证查询延迟忽略不计的前提下，选择召回率与QPS折中最优的算法。该模型通过回归分析（而非分类）进行训练，并只使用从22个特征中筛选出的3个关键特征。

Result: 该路由框架在5个未见过的验证数据集上，相比现有的过滤ANN基准，实现了最先进的召回率与QPS平衡。

Conclusion: 通过查询感知路由框架，能够根据具体的查询和谓词动态选择最优的过滤ANN算法，在多个数据集上实现了召回率与QPS的最佳平衡，且几乎没有引入额外的延迟。

Abstract: Filtered ANN search, which combines vector similarity with attribute predicates, is a core primitive in modern vector databases and retrieval-augmented generation. We benchmark all major categorical filtered ANN methods across multiple datasets under three predicates and find that no single method dominates. Moreover, even within a single dataset and predicate type, the best method for a query can vary. Therefore, we propose a query-aware routing framework. A lightweight ML model predicts each candidate method's recall on the query, and the router consults an offline benchmark table that maps every method and parameter setting to its measured recall and QPS, then selects the method with the best recall--QPS trade-off. Our ablation study narrows 22 candidate features to a minimal set of three and we adopt regression rather than classification as the prediction target to sharpen accuracy. Our model is trained on six real-world datasets and applied to five unseen validation datasets. The final result shows that our router achieves state-of-the-art recall and QPS balance across all five validation datasets compared to existing filtered ANN baselines, while incurring negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [258] [The Bi-Channel Networking Paradigm for Database Systems in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19969)
*Georg Kreuzmayr,Muhammad El-Hindi,Benjamin Wagner,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对高速网络下内核TCP堆栈性能瓶颈问题，本文提出了“双通道范式”，通过将数据库数据平面和控制平面分别对接用户态UDP和内核态TCP，在保证可靠性的同时实现了极致的性能与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高速云网络环境下，传统数据库依赖操作系统内核TCP堆栈作为“黑盒”导致了严重的CPU开销瓶颈；而完全弃用TCP转向用户态UDP又难以解决可靠性和有序性问题。

Method: 提出了“双通道范式”（bi-channel paradigm），将通信分为：1. 面向延迟和带宽敏感操作的高性能数据通道（基于用户态UDP）；2. 面向协调和恢复的可靠控制通道（基于内核态TCP）。

Result: 在分布式Shuffle任务中，仅用3个CPU核心即实现了200 Gbit/s的饱和传输；在复制键值存储中，实现了每秒数百万次的消息处理能力。

Conclusion: 通过将数据平面与控制平面分离，针对不同的数据库操作采用最合适的网络协议（UDP/TCP），可以有效克服内核TCP堆栈在高带宽网络下的性能瓶颈，实现高吞吐量与低延迟。

Abstract: When network links were slow, cloud and distributed database systems could rely on generic kernel abstractions and treat network communication as a black box. With today's fast cloud networks, this approach breaks down: database performance becomes limited by the CPU overhead of the kernel TCP stack. Replacing TCP with user-space UDP can reduce this overhead, but it requires reimplementing essential guarantees, such as reliability and ordering. To solve this conundrum, database systems should no longer treat networking as a black box but co-design it with database operations. We propose the bi-channel paradigm for database systems, which separates communication into two channels: A high-performance data path for latency- and bandwidth-sensitive operations, and a reliable control path for coordination and recovery. We implement the paradigm by combining user-space UDP and kernel-based TCP, though other stack combinations are possible. This design exploits modern NIC capabilities while preserving TCP's reliability. We demonstrate the paradigm's efficiency and simplicity in two representative settings: a distributed shuffle saturating 200 Gbit/s with three CPU cores, and a replicated key-value store processing millions of messages per second.

</details>


### [259] [Caching for Dollars, Not Hits: An Exact Offline Reference for Cloud-Egress Caching and the Crossover That Decides When It Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20539)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了云存储场景下的成本感知缓存问题，提出了一种衡量缓存策略相对于“美元最优”差距的基准，证明了传统的最小缺失率策略在计费场景下是次优的，并提供了评估策略选择的理论依据和数学阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储的计费模式（基于请求和流量）与传统缓存策略追求的目标（最小化命中缺失率）不符，导致昂贵对象的存取成本远超频繁但廉价的对象，缺乏衡量现有启发式算法与最优美元成本差距的基准。

Method: 1. 针对固定大小页面，利用积分区间线性规划计算离线美元最优解；2. 针对变量大小页面，扩展了基于流的离线界限（cost-FOO）以适应美元成本；3. 建立了可重现的账单忠实基准，评估LRU和GreedyDual等启发式算法的美元后悔值（dollar-regret）。

Result: 1. 揭示了异构后悔定律：LRU的成本后悔随miss cost的离散度增加而剧增；2. 识别了争用边界：当预算覆盖昂贵的工作集时，GreedyDual几乎达到最优；3. 推导了闭式交叉阈值（s*）：通过GET费用与流出率的比值，预测不同云部署是否需要使用代价感知缓存。

Conclusion: 传统的缓存策略（关注最小化缺失率）在面对现代云存储定价结构（按请求次数和流量计费）时是不够的。研究提出了基于成本的优化理论和评估框架，证明了在异构成本环境下，贪婪算法（如GreedyDual）能有效降低美元代价，并导出了预测是否需要进行代价感知缓存的阈值公式。

Abstract: When a cache miss fetches from cloud object storage, the bill is per GET request and per byte of egress, not latency. Classic caching minimizes the miss rate, the wrong objective: a rarely but expensively fetched object can cost thousands of times more dollars than a frequently but cheaply fetched one. Generalized-caching theory bounds the miss-cost objective, but no reported benchmark measures how far deployed heuristics sit from the dollar-optimal offline policy on real cloud prices. We supply that reference. For uniform-size page caches with heterogeneous miss costs the offline dollar-optimum is exact in polynomial time via an integral interval linear program -- validated against brute force; variable sizes are NP-hard, so we extend the flow-based offline bound from the hit-ratio objective to dollars (cost-FOO), tight to about four percent. Against this reference we find: (i) a heterogeneity-regret law -- LRU's dollar-regret rises with miss-cost dispersion (Spearman 0.87) while cost-aware GreedyDual cuts it to roughly a tenth; (ii) a contention frontier -- GreedyDual's residual regret collapses to near zero exactly when the budget fits the expensive working set, and is the open slice otherwise; and (iii) a closed-form crossover s* = GET_fee/egress_rate (about 4 KB on S3, 330 B on GCS) that predicts which deployments need dollar-aware caching at all. On a real Twitter trace the price vector alone moves the workload across s*, shifting the regime as predicted. The artifact is a reproducible billing-faithful benchmark; heuristics and bounds it builds on are prior work, credited.

</details>


### [260] [AgenticDB: Agentic Performance Reconfiguration for Database Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20318)
*Xinyue Yang,Chaozheng Wang,Chen Zheng,Heng Zhang,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AgenticDB 是一种用于数据库工作负载重配置的代理式框架，通过实时反馈与跨任务经验学习，在 DBMS 和操作系统层面自动执行更安全、更高效的配置优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调优工具多局限于 DBMS 参数的迭代搜索，导致执行成本高、搜索空间受限，且难以处理真实场景中诸如运行时瓶颈诊断、操作系统层面的配置及执行安全性等实际问题。

Method: 提出 AgenticDB 智能体框架。该框架通过上下文引导的交互机制，综合考量 DBMS 和 OS 级别的配置选项；结合安全约束进行变更执行、实时监控性能与系统状态，并利用反馈循环实现自我优化与经验积累。

Result: 在 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 上，通过 YCSB、Sysbench 和 TPC-H 工作负载评估，AgenticDB 在所有测试中均达到最优性能，较最佳基线提升了 118.1%，调优时间缩短了 22.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticDB 成功将数据库调优转换为一个具备自我改进能力的代理式过程。实验证明，引入操作系统级调优空间、稳健的执行生命周期以及基于记忆的规划机制，显著提升了数据库配置的效果与调优的实用性。

Abstract: Database configuration tuning is critical for workload performance, but practical tuning on real deployments remains difficult. Existing automatic tuners mostly formulate tuning as iterative search over DBMS knob values. This formulation leads to high execution cost, prematurely narrows the configuration space, and leaves practical requirements insufficiently addressed: diagnosing runtime bottlenecks from system feedback, exploring OS-level reconfiguration opportunities, executing changes robustly, and learning from previous trials and tasks.
  We propose AgenticDB, an agentic framework for database workload reconfiguration. AgenticDB implements a context-grounded harness that interacts with the target database environment by proposing DBMS- and OS-level changes, applying them under safety constraints, observing workload performance and runtime states, and using execution feedback to guide subsequent decisions. This runtime interaction enables AgenticDB to diagnose bottlenecks, explore a broader DBMS- and OS-level reconfiguration space, avoid unsafe or unsupported actions, and accumulate experience within and across reconfiguration tasks. As a result, AgenticDB turns database tuning into a self-refining reconfiguration process in which runtime feedback iteratively improves later decisions.
  We conduct extensive experiments on MySQL and PostgreSQL using YCSB, Sysbench, and TPC-H workloads. The results show that AgenticDB achieves the best final performance on all evaluated workloads, improving over the strongest baseline by 118.1% on average and reducing aggregate time-to-best by 22.6%. The results also demonstrate that its OS-level action space, robust execution lifecycle, and memory-enhanced planning contribute to more effective and practical database reconfiguration.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [MonaVec: A Training-Free Embedded Vector Search Kernel for Edge and Offline AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19458)
*Oğuzhan Yenen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MonaVec是一款专为边缘和离线AI设计的确定性向量搜索内核，特点是无需数据训练、开箱即用，通过RHDH量化和确定性哈希，在极小空间内实现了媲美大型框架的召回性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量检索系统多依赖服务器环境、大内存或预先训练，无法满足边缘设备、离线环境或对结果确定性有严苛要求的应用场景需求。

Method: 使用随机哈达玛变换（RHDH）将任意输入分布规范化为标准正态分布，从而实现无需训练的数据无关（data-oblivious）4位量化；采用纯Rust实现，支持SIMD优化，并结合ChaCha20种子实现跨架构确定性结果。

Result: 在45K规模的语义嵌入测试中，MonaVec以27MB的存储占用实现了0.960的Recall@10，超越了float32 FAISS-IVF和8-bit usearch的召回率表现，并提供字节级一致的确定性。

Conclusion: MonaVec提供了一种针对边缘和离线AI场景的确定性向量搜索方案，凭借其“单文件、单函数调用”的特性及无需训练的量子化技术，成功填补了轻量级、可移植向量检索的空白。

Abstract: We present MonaVec, a deterministic, embedded vector-search kernel for edge and offline AI -- settings where server infrastructure, network connectivity, and training data are all unavailable. Existing vector-search systems assume a persistent server, gigabytes of RAM, or a training pass over the corpus; MonaVec instead targets the deployment profile of SQLite: one file, one function call, runs anywhere. Its quantization core is training-free by default and data-oblivious: a Randomized Hadamard Transform (RHDH) conditions any input distribution toward N(0,1), so precomputed Lloyd-Max tables quantize to 4 bits (8x smaller) with no learned codebook and no data pass. The index persists as a single .mvec file whose embedded ChaCha20 rotation seed makes results reproducible across architectures and byte-identical within a build -- a determinism guarantee that parallel-build graph libraries cannot offer.
  On semantic embeddings (AG News, 45K x 1024-dim BGE-M3, cosine), MonaVec 4-bit BruteForce reaches 0.960 Recall@10 in 27 MB -- leading float32 FAISS-IVF and 8-bit usearch on recall -- while trading peak throughput for byte-identical determinism. A single-pass global standardization (fit()) extends the same data-oblivious pipeline to magnitude-sensitive L2 data, and optional IvfFlat and HNSW backends carry it to million-vector corpora.
  MonaVec is implemented in pure Rust with Python bindings and runtime SIMD dispatch (AVX-512/AVX2/NEON/scalar). It targets on-device RAG, offline agents, and embedded retrieval -- the niche SQLite occupies for relational data: one file, one call, runs anywhere.

</details>


### [262] [VCG: A Multimodal Retrieval Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds under Extreme Cold-Start Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19627)
*Katya Mirylenka,Egor Malykh,Mahdyar Ravanbakhsh,Michael Gygli,Marco-Andrea Buchmann,Andrew Dzhoha,Svitlana Borzenko,Francesca Catino,Mohamed Gaafar,Maarten Versteegh,Thomas Kober,Dario d'Andrea,Ellie Langhans*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 VCG 视频检索系统，利用领域自适应 CLIP 技术解决电商短视频流面临的冷启动与交互偏差问题，实现了高效的零样本语义检索，并显著提升了视频互动指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电商场景中，视频流取代了传统搜索目录，引发了“极端冷启动”问题（新视频缺乏交互数据），同时传统的协同过滤算法受到位置和时长偏见的影响，无法准确反映内容价值。

Method: 1. 构建了基于领域自适应 CLIP 的视频候选生成系统 (VCG)，将视频和用户映射到共享语义空间进行零样本检索；2. 对比了生成式 (LLM) 与判别式 (CLIP) 嵌入在检索任务中的表现；3. 采用了多模态检索架构来处理极端冷启动并对抗参与度偏差。

Result: 1. 在检索任务中，生成式模型出现了嵌入空间崩溃（Embedding Space Collapse）现象；2. 判别式模型（CLIP）的表现优于 LLM；3. 在线 A/B 测试显示，VCG 促使视频深度完成度提升了 50%。

Conclusion: VCG 通过利用领域自适应的视听语言模型（基于 CLIP），在无需用户交互历史的情况下实现了高质量的短视频检索。实验表明判别式模型（CLIP）在检索任务中优于生成式模型（LLM），且该系统成功降低了评估偏差，显著提升了用户深层交互指标。

Abstract: The digital commerce landscape is shifting from static, search-driven catalogs to dynamic, immersive video feeds. This transition introduces an ``extreme cold-start'' problem: unlike traditional items, new short-form videos lack the dense interaction history required for collaborative filtering. Furthermore, immersive feeds introduce strong position and duration biases that distort standard engagement signals. In this paper, we demonstrate the Video Candidate Generation (VCG) system, a scalable multimodal retrieval engine designed to solve these challenges in a large-scale e-commerce environment. By leveraging a domain-adapted vision-language model (based on CLIP), we map users and videos into a shared semantic space, enabling zero-shot retrieval based on visual content rather than behavioral history. We detail the system's architecture and present a rigorous evaluation comparing generative (LLM) vs. discriminative (CLIP) embeddings. Our results show that while generative models excel at attribute prediction, they suffer from embedding space collapse in retrieval tasks. Online A/B testing demonstrates that VCG effectively mitigates engagement biases, yielding a 50\% uplift in deep video completion. To showcase the system's capabilities, we present an interactive demonstration featuring three bi-directional retrieval scenarios: Product-to-Video, Video-to-Product, and Zero-Shot Semantic Search.

</details>


### [263] [Token Factory: Efficiently Integrating Diverse Signals into Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19635)
*Xilun Chen,Shao-Chuan Wang,Baykal Cakici,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Raghu Keshavan,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Xinyang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Token Factory 通过将传统特征映射为“软 token”，解决了大规模推荐模型中异构特征处理效率低下和序列长度爆炸的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于 Transformer 的大规模推荐模型中，如何高效、有效地集成传统特征是一个挑战。传统的“文本化”或离散表示方法会导致提示词过长、内存占用过高及计算开销巨大的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Token Factory”的框架，将各类传统推荐信号（异构特征）转换为模型可以直接处理的“软 token”（soft tokens），而非通过传统的文本化或离散表示。

Result: 实验表明，Token Factory 有效解决了提示词长度爆炸的问题，并在生产环境的推荐任务中提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: Token Factory 为在大规模推荐模型（LRMs）中高效融合传统特征提供了一种有效方案，通过将特征转化为“软 token”，在控制序列长度和计算开销的同时，提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Large Recommendation Models (LRMs) have demonstrated promising capabilities in industry-scale recommendation tasks. However, holistically integrating traditional signals into these transformer-based architectures effectively and efficiently remains a major challenge. Conventional approaches that "textualize" these signals directly or create discrete item representations often lead to excessively long prompts, substantial memory footprints, and high computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose "Token Factory", a framework designed to transform traditional signals into "soft tokens" that can be directly processed by LRMs. This approach enables efficient integration and compression of heterogeneous input features, preventing prompt length explosion while enhancing model performance. We detail the architecture of Token Factory and present experimental results validating its effectiveness in a production-scale recommendation environment.

</details>


### [264] [SAFE-Cascade: Cost-Adaptive Vision-Language Routing for Chart Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19646)
*Ayush Dwivedi,Qixin Wang,Ashvi Soni,Ruoteng Wang,Han Li,Animesh Mahapatra,Neeraj Agrawal,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAFE-Cascade 是一种针对图表问答的成本自适应交互系统，通过 OCR+轻量级文本模型并行决策，仅在必要时调用高成本 VLM，在维持高精度的同时显着降低了计算成本与延迟，并提供了高度透明的调试界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于直接对所有查询调用视觉语言模型（VLM）计算成本高昂，且许多图表问题仅依赖 OCR 文本和基础推理即可回答，因此需要一种成本自适应的策略来优化资源分配。

Method: 提出了一种交互式级联系统：首先通过 OCR 提取文本，利用轻量级纯文本大模型（GPT-4o-mini）生成初步答案，随后由基于随机森林（Random Forest）的学习型路由器决定是直接采信文本答案，还是升级调用 VLM（Gemini 1.5 Flash）进行视觉推理。系统支持用户自定义调整升级阈值以平衡成本与精度。

Result: 在 ChartQA 数据集上，SAFE-Cascade 实现了 69.1% 的统一准确率（VLM 调用率为 73.1%），对比全 VLM 基线（67.7% 准确率，100% 调用率），在性能相当的前提下显著提升了推理效率和成本效益。

Conclusion: SAFE-Cascade 通过选择性模态路由机制，成功在保持与全 VLM 基线相当性能的同时，显着降低了 VLM 的调用频率（26.9%）和预估成本（9.3%），验证了该架构在图表问答任务中的高效性与透明度。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful for chart question answering, but invoking a VLM for every query can be unnecessarily expensive when many questions are answerable from OCR text and lightweight language reasoning. We demonstrate SAFE-Cascade, an interactive system for cost-adaptive chart question answering. Given a chart image and a natural-language question, SAFE-Cascade first extracts chart text with OCR, obtains a provisional answer from a text-only language model, and then uses a learned router to decide whether to accept the text answer or escalate to a VLM. The demo exposes this decision process to users: OCR evidence, text-only answer, routing probability, escalation decision, final answer, estimated cost, and estimated latency are shown side by side. SAFE-Cascade is designed as a transparent interface for understanding when visual grounding is actually needed. Users can upload or select charts, ask questions, inspect the evidence used by each pathway, compare text-only and VLM answers, and adjust the escalation threshold to explore the accuracy-cost frontier. The system is implemented with Azure Document Intelligence for OCR, gpt-5-mini as the text-only model, gemini-2.5-flash-image as the VLM, and a Random Forest router trained on inference-time features. On a held-out ChartQA test split of 375 examples from a 2,500-example experiment, SAFE-Cascade achieves 69.1% unified accuracy with 73.1% VLM invocation, compared with 67.7% accuracy and 100% VLM invocation for the full-VLM baseline. The observed +1.4 percentage-point difference is statistically uncertain, so we interpret SAFE-Cascade as matching full-VLM performance while reducing VLM calls by 26.9% and estimated cost by 9.3%. The demonstration shows how selective modality routing can make multimodal knowledge systems more transparent, tunable, and cost-aware.

</details>


### [265] [Closing the Calibration Gap in Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19719)
*Aditeya Baral,Radoslav Ralev,Iliya Sotirov Zhechev,Srijith Rajamohan,Jen Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出语义缓存评估中 PR-AUC 指标的局限性，提出了一套新的缓存感知评估框架（P-CHR 和 CRR），并证明了优化缓存效果的关键在于解决模型的校准问题而非仅仅优化排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存评估指标 PR-AUC 忽略了固定阈值下的可用性，导致高 PR-AUC 的模型在实际部署时表现往往不佳，难以指导高效的模型选择。

Method: 1. 引入了 P-CHR (Precision-Cache Hit Ratio) AUC 评价指标，在不同缓存利用率下衡量精度；2. 提出了 CRR (Calibration Retention Rate) 以量化离线排序质量在部署中的保持程度；3. 将离线与部署间的性能差距分解为可恢复的校准分量和不可约的结构分量。

Result: 研究发现校准差距主要取决于训练目标而非数据规模，且事后校准（post-hoc calibration）只能部分弥补性能差距，证明了现有指标与实际部署需求存在严重失配。

Conclusion: 语义缓存的模型选择应被视为校准问题而非单纯的排序问题。衡量缓存性能需要采用新的评价框架（P-CHR和CRR）来弥合离线评估与生产部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Semantic caching cuts LLM inference costs by serving a cached response to semantically similar queries. Standard practice evaluates these systems using PR-AUC, a metric that only measures how well scores rank and ignores whether they are usable at a fixed threshold. We show this mismatch leads to systematically poor deployment choices, as models with the highest PR-AUC are often the worst in operation. We introduce Precision-Cache Hit Ratio (P-CHR) AUC, a cache-aware metric that measures precision across cache utilization levels, and Calibration Retention Rate (CRR), which captures how much offline ranking quality survives at deployment. We decompose the operational gap between offline and deployed quality into a recoverable calibration component and an irreducible structural component fixed by the dataset's positive rate. Our experiments show that the calibration gap is governed by the training objective rather than data scale, and post-hoc calibration only partially closes it. Ultimately, model selection for semantic caching is a calibration problem, not a ranking one, and measuring it is the first step to closing the gap.

</details>


### [266] [Stellar: Scalable Multimodal Document Retrieval for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19960)
*Yuxiang Guo,Zhonghao Hu,Yuren Mao,Yuhang Liu,Congcong Ge,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Stellar是一个可扩展的多模态检索框架，通过基于MLLM的词法过滤和优化的磁盘存储加载机制，解决了多向量检索面临的高内存开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多模态文档检索方法依赖多向量表示和后期交互（late interaction），在处理大规模语料库时存在巨大的内存开销，制约了其在实际RAG系统中的可扩展性和部署能力。

Method: 提出了Stellar框架，包含两个核心组件：1. 基于词法表示的过滤（LRF）：微调多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为稀疏编码器，进行高效的初步文档筛选；2. 高效磁盘后期交互（DLI）：设计了基于平衡聚类算法的磁盘存储布局，并引入了基于代价模型的动态内存加载机制。

Result: 在四个真实数据集及一个新提出的大规模数据集上的实验表明，Stellar在不牺牲检索有效性的前提下，将内存开销和查询延迟降低了1到2个数量级。

Conclusion: Stellar通过将多模态文档检索中的内存消耗从存储瓶颈中解放出来，实现了高效率与高扩展性。它证明了在保持检索精确度的同时，通过精细的过滤机制和磁盘存储布局优化，可以显著降低现实场景下的部署门槛。

Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval--selecting the most relevant multimodal document from a large corpus to answer a natural language query--plays an essential role in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. State-of-the-art methods represent each document and query with multiple token-level embeddings and use late interaction to achieve high effectiveness. However, such multi-vector representations incur substantial memory overhead during retrieval, leading to poor scalability and hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we present Stellar, a scalable multimodal document retrieval framework that stores token-level document embeddings on disk and loads only a small set of candidate embeddings into memory for late interaction. Stellar comprises two key components: (i) Lexical Representation-based Filtering (LRF), which fine-tunes a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a sparse encoder to produce high-quality lexical representations, enabling efficient and effective document filtering to significantly reduce the candidate set; (ii) Efficient Disk-backed Late Interaction (DLI), which designs an on-disk token embedding storage layout guided by a balanced clustering algorithm, and dynamically loads only the necessary token embeddings into memory using a simple yet effective cost model. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks and a newly presented large-scale dataset demonstrate that Stellar reduces memory overhead and query latency by 1-2 orders of magnitude compared to existing methods without compromising retrieval effectiveness.

</details>


### [267] [PACMS: Submodular Context Selection as a Pluggable Engine for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20047)
*Manu Ghulyani,Arunabh Singh,Karan Bharadwaj,Ankit Nath,Suranjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 智能体当前的上下文削减策略（基于近期性）存在严重缺陷，无法根据查询相关性保留关键历史信息，本文呼吁将对话、记忆与工具输出整合为统一的候选池，并根据即时需求进行动态相关性筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型（LLM）智能体在交互过程中，上下文窗口被用户对话、记忆存储和大量工具调用输出迅速填满。现有的“近期性削减”策略往往会丢失关键的早期信息，导致长跨度对话中信息回溯失败。

Method: 通过批判性分析，指出现有的RAG（检索增强生成）和上下文压缩方法均未将记忆条目、对话轮次和工具输出视为一个统一的候选池进行按需筛选。作者暗示了需要一种全新的机制，能够基于即时查询的相关性来动态决定上下文的保留与丢弃。

Result: 揭示了现有上下文管理策略（如近期性截断、简单的摘要、外部RAG或非查询敏感的压缩方法）在面对复杂工具使用及长程对话记忆时的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的基于“近期性”的上下文削减机制（recency truncation）在面对需要跨多轮对话进行信息回溯的智能体任务时存在缺陷，无法实现上下文的语义相关性最优分配。

Abstract: Conversational and tool-using LLM agents operate over a context window that fills from several directions simultaneously. As a session proceeds, the agent accumulates user and assistant turns, entries drawn from a persistent memory store, and often largest of all, the verbatim outputs of tool calls such as file reads, search results, and API responses. Once the cumulative context exceeds the model's token budget, the framework must decide what to keep.
  The prevailing mechanism is recency truncation, sometimes paired with periodic summarization. This is topic-blind: a fact established early in a session is discarded simply because it is old, even when the current user query is about exactly that fact; conversely, verbose but irrelevant recent material is retained. Agents that must recall information across many turns, the defining case for memory, are precisely where recency truncation fails.
  Existing alternatives sit outside the agent's assembly step. Retrieval augmented generation fetches external documents into the prompt but does not arbitrate the agent's \emph{already-present} pooled context. Context-compression methods reduce token count by rewriting or pruning text, but operate query-blind and lossily. Neither treats memory entries, conversation turns, and tool outputs as a single candidate pool to be selected from by relevance at the moment the prompt is assembled.

</details>


### [268] [Generative Engine Optimization at Scale: Measuring Brand Visibility Across AI Search Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20065)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 由于用户搜索习惯向AI工具转移，SEO正演变为GEO。该研究通过分析大规模数据发现，品牌在AI中的曝光度严重依赖品牌体量，且“best-of”榜单为最具协同效应的引文策略，研究首次为衡量AI品牌可见度建立了定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户习惯转向AI驱动的搜索引擎（如ChatGPT, Perplexity等），传统的SEO已不再足够。研究旨在理解AI如何呈现、引用和推荐品牌，即“生成式搜索引擎优化（GEO）”，以帮助中小企业及非头部品牌在AI搜索环境中提升可见度。

Method: 利用Ranqo平台追踪100多个品牌，收集并分析了2026年3月至5月期间超过10万条AI搜索提示响应数据，评估品牌在AI回答中的出现频率、引文渠道及情感倾向。

Result: 1. 品牌可见度呈现三级阶梯：全球知名品牌出现率为73%，中型品牌为44%，小型品牌仅为11%。2. 搜索引擎偏好：78%的引用指向企业官网，非企业源中YouTube排首位。3. 内容形式：排名前列的“best-of”列表类文章是最高效的引文格式（占比21%）。4. 舆情波动：品牌被提及的频率相对稳定，但情感倾向（正面或负面）的波动频率是提及频率的6.7倍。

Conclusion: AI搜索引擎中的品牌可见度（GEO）是可量化的，且与品牌成熟度紧密相关；企业需从传统的SEO转型为GEO，关注被引用的内容形式（如“best-of”榜单）和信源偏好，以提升AI搜索引擎中的可见度。

Abstract: People increasingly get answers straight from AI search engines like ChatGPT, Claude, Perplexity, and Gemini rather than scrolling search results. Brands that once focused on search engine optimization (SEO) must now optimize for how these engines represent, cite, and recommend them -- a shift variously called Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), Answer Engine Optimization (AEO), and AI Search Visibility. We treat AEO and AI Visibility as part of GEO, and study how to measure brand visibility across AI engines: what they value when they cite a brand, which sources they rely on, and what content large language models surface. The hard case is everyone outside the already-authoritative top brands -- SMEs, D2C brands, creators, and early-stage startups.
  We analyze 100K+ prompt responses across 100+ brands tracked on Ranqo between March and May 2026. First visibility runs form a clear three-tier brand-stature ladder: global household names (e.g., Stripe, Nike) appear in 73% of relevant AI answers on their first run; established mid-market and regional brands (e.g., Olipop, Klaviyo) in 44%; niche and small brands in just 11% -- about 30 percentage points per step. When engines cite sources, about 78% go to corporate websites; among non-corporate sources YouTube leads, ahead of Reddit, editorial media, and Wikipedia. The highest-leverage page is the ranked "best-of" listicle, the most-cited content format at about 21% of all citations. Sentiment is the unstable signal: whether a brand is framed positively or negatively flips about 6.7 times more often than whether it is mentioned at all.
  These findings provide a first large-scale baseline for measuring GEO: AI brand visibility can be measured, differs by platform, and varies strongly by brand maturity. We close by proposing seven v1.1 protocols to test whether specific recommendations can causally improve AI visibility.

</details>


### [269] [ScholarQuest: A Taxonomy-Guided Benchmark for Agentic Academic Paper Search in Open Literature Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20235)
*Tingyue Pan,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Yitong Zhou,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScholarQuest，这是一个针对学术文献搜索智能体的大规模分类指导基准，旨在解决现有评估体系不足的问题，实验证明当前学术搜索智能体虽有优势，但仍远未达到理想水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试无法系统性评估真实开放文献环境下基于LLM的搜索智能体，且缺乏针对复杂、迭代式学术检索的评估方案。

Method: 构建了ScholarQuest基准测试，涵盖1000多个计算机科学主题和四种代表性检索意图（方法导向、设定锚定、对比基准、范围受限），并开发了共享检索后端ScholarBase。

Result: 实验表明，智能体方法优于传统的单次检索基线，但最佳智能体的Recall@100仅为0.314，Recall@All为0.355，表明准确性仍有很大提升潜力。同时，分析揭示了智能体在搜索效率和意图鲁棒性方面的短板。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM驱动学术搜索智能体表现仍有较大提升空间。ScholarQuest成功揭示了智能体在复杂学术检索任务中的局限性，并为多维度评估提供了标准化的基准。

Abstract: Academic paper search is a core step in scientific research, and LLM-based search agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for iterative, intent-driven literature exploration. However, existing benchmarks are insufficient for systematically evaluating agentic academic search under realistic open literature environments. We propose ScholarQuest, a large-scale, taxonomy-guided benchmark for agentic academic paper search. ScholarQuest is constructed from over 1,000 computer science topics and four representative research intents, including method-oriented, setting-anchored, comparison-based, and scope-controlled queries. It further provides scalable answer construction and a shared retrieval backend ScholarBase for reproducible evaluation. Benchmarking results show that agentic methods outperform single-shot retrieval baselines, yet the best-performing agent only achieves 0.314 Recall@100 and 0.355 Recall@All, indicating substantial room for improvement. In addition, analyses of search efficiency, intent-level robustness, and failure cases further highlight the benchmark's ability to provide multi-dimensional evaluation signals for academic paper search agents.

</details>


### [270] [ELVA: Exploring Ranking-Driven Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20280)
*Yuhan Liu,Pei Fu,Hang Li,Yukun Qi,Chao Jiang,Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Bin Qin,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Jingmin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多模态检索中“粒度盲视”问题，本文提出了ELVA框架，通过无需奖励模型的强化学习与规则奖励机制，精准优化负样本排序，有效提升了模型处理复杂多粒度查询的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于对比学习的多模态大模型检索方法存在“粒度盲视”（grain blindness）问题，即无法充分处理复杂查询中关键的粒度信息，且传统对比学习将负样本简单处理为非正样本，忽略了负样本间的信息差异。

Method: 1. 提出ELVA框架，将基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）引入检索任务，在无显式排序标签的情况下优化检索行为；2. 设计基于规则的奖励机制，用于优化负样本排序并扩大正负样本间的相似度间隙；3. 构建MRBench基准测试集，专门用于评估复杂的多粒度查询场景。

Result: ELVA在各类标准检索基准测试中均达到了当前最优水平（SOTA），特别是在针对性的MRBench评估中取得了13.1%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ELVA框架通过基于规则的强化学习缓解了通用多模态检索中的“粒度盲视”问题，不仅在标准数据集上达到了SOTA，且在新构建的MRBench上表现显著，证实了其改进检索精细度的有效性。

Abstract: Leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) via contrastive learning has become a mainstream paradigm for improving the performance of Universal Multimodal Retrieval (UMR). However, previous works have ignored the grain blindness when adapting the contrastive paradigm into retrieval tasks. Grain blindness refers to the tendency of the model to overlook grain-level information contained in the query, which is crucial for effectively handling complex queries. This stems from contrastive learning treating samples as a binary classification (positive/negative), while ignoring the different information carried by each negative sample. To address this, we argue that negatives should be treated differently according to their similarity to the positive sample, enabling the model to learn distinct grain information from each negative. In this paper, we introduce a simple but effective framework, called ELVA, a novel rule-based RL framework that mitigates grain blindness through ranking-driven MLLMs. 1) Instead of relying on reward models, we extend Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards
  (RLVR) to retrieval tasks, allowing the model to explore new ranking behaviors without explicit ranking labels. 2) By utilizing rule-based rewards, our approach jointly optimizes the ranking of negative samples while enlarging the similarity gap between positive and negative. To more precisely measure grain blindness, we further introduce MRBench, a new benchmark specifically designed for multi-grain query scenarios. ELVA achieves state-of-the-art results across standard retrieval benchmarks, and its notable 13.1% improvement on MRBench further demonstrates its effectiveness in alleviating grain blindness.

</details>


### [271] [Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20554)
*Ruizhong Qiu,Yinglong Xia,Dongqi Fu,Hanqing Zeng,Ren Chen,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐在复杂上下文建模上的不足，G2Rec通过结合图协同参与建模与语义标记，实现了在大规模工业场景下无需标注的精准用户兴趣建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型在处理复杂用户行为和语义上下文时存在局限：图方法扩展性差或仅能利用局部信息，而语义标记方法往往依赖启发式规则且缺乏显式监督，导致语义表示次优。

Method: 提出了G2Rec框架，将整体图协同参与建模（holistic graph-based co-engagement modeling）与语义标记（semantic tokenization）相结合，无需基准用户兴趣，实现了用户兴趣原型的捕捉。

Result: 在工业级产品界面部署及多个公开数据集上的实验表明，G2Rec表现优于现有方法，提升了推荐的准确性与综合性。

Conclusion: G2Rec通过整合全局图结构信息与语义标记，有效解决了生成式推荐中兴趣建模的局限性，在工业级应用中实现了更准确的用户行为上下文建模，证明了该范式的优越性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [SPINE: A Fault Injection Profiler for Quantized Neural Networks under Accumulated Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19526)
*Nathan Guimarães,Ian Kersz,Leonardo R. Gobatto,Fabio Benevenuti,Michael G. Jordan,Antonio Carlos S. Beck,Fernanda L. Kastensmidt,Jose Rodrigo Azambuja*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对边缘设备上量化神经网络的故障注入评估框架，通过在二进制层面分析内存内的累积权重位翻转，为优化边缘神经网络的可靠性提供了策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的故障注入研究未能全面描述量化神经网络在边缘设备真实内存布局下，累积故障如何转化为预测错误，导致边缘部署难以在严苛环境下保证效率与可靠性的平衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于GDB驱动的性能分析框架，可在无需重新训练或修改代码的情况下，直接向目标边缘CPU二进制文件注入累积权重位翻转，并生成分层的故障配置文件。

Result: 通过对多种拓扑结构、量化水平和内存布局的评估，揭示了故障分布与网络架构及内存布局之间的关联规律，证明了Selective Hardening的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过针对性保护（Selective Hardening）即能以较低的代价有效提升量化神经网络在边缘设备上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks at the edge demands efficient inference under strict cost and power constraints. Quantized neural networks address these demands by replacing floating-point parameters with low-precision integers, yet their weights remain continuously exposed to radiation-induced bit-flips during inference. Fault Injection can be used to simulate those environments, but existing studies fail to characterize how accumulated upsets translate into mispredictions under realistic memory layouts. This paper presents a GDB-driven profiling framework that injects cumulative weight bit-flips directly onto the target binary of edge CPUs, generating per-layer fault profiles without requiring model retraining or code modification. Evaluated across multiple topologies, quantization efforts, and memory layouts, the results indicate how selective hardening strategies should be applied to effectively protect neural networks.

</details>


### [273] [A Tool for the Synthesis of Adaptive Probabilistic Processors Based on the Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19533)
*Jonathan Juracy Carneiro da Silva,Leonardo R. Gobatto,Jose Rodrigo Azambuja*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 开发了一款用于组合优化问题Ising映射的自动化仿真工具，通过自适应算法选择和架构参数配置，显著提升了概率计算的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题映射至Ising模型时，手动配置过于繁琐，且缺乏针对不同硬件实现策略的系统化评估工具与自适应参数选择机制。

Method: 提出了一套自动化工具，可根据问题规模和拓扑结构自动构建Ising哈密顿量，确定p-bit数量，并具备自适应策略，可从Gibbs采样、模拟退火(SA)、模拟量子退火(SQA)及基于簇的算法中选择最优更新算法。

Result: 在基准问题测试中，该工具表现出比固定更新方法更好的收敛行为和更高的适应性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了组合优化问题的求解灵活性与收敛性能，为基于MTJ和p-bit的概率计算硬件开发及其系统化评估提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: This work presents a tool for the synthesis and simulation of probabilistic architectures for solving combinatorial optimization problems by mapping them to the Ising model. The proposed approach automatically constructs the Ising Hamiltonian and determines the number of probabilistic elements (p-bits) based on problem characteristics such as size and topology. Furthermore, the tool introduces an adaptive strategy for selecting the most suitable update algorithm among Gibbs Sampling, Simulated Annealing (SA), Simulated Quantum Annealing (SQA), and cluster-based methods. Experimental results using benchmark problems demonstrate improved convergence behavior and flexibility compared to fixed approaches. The proposed framework enables systematic evaluation of probabilistic computing strategies and supports the development of future hardware implementations based on MTJs and p-bits.

</details>


### [274] [Design and Evaluation of Energy-Efficient Whisper Dot-Product Kernel Offloading on a CGLA Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19913)
*Takuto Ando,Yu Eto,Ayumu Takeuchi,Yasuhiko Nakashima*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 IMAX 的可编程粗粒度线性阵列架构，专门用于加速 Whisper ASR 模型的点积计算，通过软硬协同优化在 tiny 模型下显著降低了功耗延迟积 (PDP)，优于主流边缘 AI 平台和高性能 GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper 模型在通用处理器（如 ARM Cortex-A72）上存在计算瓶颈，点积运算占用极高比例的 FP16/Q8_0 执行时间，亟需通过定制化架构提升端侧推断的能效。

Method: 在 IMAX 架构上针对 Whisper 点积算子进行优化，包括：1. 算子映射、本地存储大小优化及脉冲调度（burst scheduling）；2. 实现 FP16 转 FP32 的内联转换、64位数据路径下的双路 SIMD FMA 操作；3. 采用列式多线程技术；4. 支持宿主机 CPU 与 IMAX 的混合执行模式（分担残差段）。

Result: 基于 28nm ASIC 工艺（840MHz）的模拟测试显示，IMAX 在运行 Whisper-tiny.en Q8_0 模型时的 PDP 为 11.58J，分别比 Jetson AGX Orin 和 RTX 4090 低 2.35 倍和 10.48 倍。随着模型增大（base/small），受限于本地内存覆盖率，其能效优势会略有缩减。

Conclusion: IMAX 架构通过可编程的粗粒度线性阵列（CGLA）有效解决了 Whisper 模型中点积计算的瓶颈，在端侧 ASR 应用中实现了优异的能效比（PDP），尤其在 tiny 模型规模下表现领先。

Abstract: In this paper, we implement and evaluate Whisper dot-product kernel offloading on IMAX, a programmable Coarse-Grained Linear Arrays (CGLAs) architecture. Whisper-tiny.en profiling on an ARM Cortex-A72 shows that dot-product operations account for 90.6% of FP16 execution time and 87.1% of Q8_0 execution time. To address this kernel bottleneck, we combine kernel mapping, local-memory sizing, and burst scheduling. The implementation uses inline FP16-to-FP32 conversion, 2-way SIMD FMA on a 64-bit datapath, column-wise multithreading, and mixed execution in which aligned vector segments run on IMAX and residual segments run concurrently on the host CPU. We evaluate the design with an FPGA prototype and a 28nm ASIC projection at 840MHz. For Whisper-tiny.en, 32KB local memory and burst length 16 jointly minimize PDP and EDP. Under a TDP-based cross-platform comparison, the projected IMAX records a PDP of 11.58J for Whisper-tiny.en Q8_0, 2.35x lower than Jetson AGX Orin (27.16J) and 10.48x lower than RTX 4090 (121.38J). The same design extends to Whisper-base.en and Whisper-small.en, where the PDP gap narrows as 32KB local-memory coverage drops from 93.8% for tiny to about 66.5% for base and small. These results position IMAX as a programmable architecture for lower-PDP local ASR in the tiny-model regime.

</details>


### [275] [ExSpike: A General Full-Event Neuromorphic Architecture for Exploiting Irregular Sparsity with Event Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20414)
*Yuehai Chen,Farhad Merchant*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ExSpike，一种通过纯事件驱动数据流优化、硬件架构设计及空间冗余压缩，充分利用 SNN 不规则稀疏性的全事件神经形态架构，大幅提升了算效表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽然具有稀疏的时空活动性，但尚缺乏有效的全事件计算架构来将这种不规则的稀疏性转化为实际的性能和能效提升。

Method: 1. 提出了一套数据流优化方案，确保 SNN 各层输入保持脉冲形式，实现纯事件驱动执行；2. 设计了支持该数据流的硬件架构 ExSpike，包含用于脉冲驱动自注意力的 Attention Core；3. 引入相邻位置事件压缩（Adjacent-position event compression）技术，减少空间相邻脉冲序列的冗余计算。

Result: 在 AMD Xilinx Virtex-7 FPGA 上实现，在分类和分割任务中表现出色；达到高达 479.15 GOPS、281.85 GOPS/W 和 0.80 GOPS/W/PE 的性能；PE 归一化能效比现有最先进（SOTA）的 FireFly-T 架构高出 10 倍。

Conclusion: ExSpike 是一种高效的通用全事件神经形态架构，能够真正利用脉冲神经网络（SNN）中不规则的稀疏性，在保持竞争性准确率的同时，显著提升能效和算力，优于现有的 FPGA SNN 加速器。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) promise energy-efficient computing due to their sparse spatio-temporal activity. However, effectively translating such irregular sparsity into practical performance and energy gains remains challenging, as full-event computing architectures are still underexplored. This paper proposes ExSpike, a general full-event neuromorphic architecture that fully exploits irregular sparsity in SNNs. To realize pure event-driven execution, we first propose a set of dataflow optimizations to ensure that the inputs to each SNN layer remain spike-based, thereby enabling full-event execution throughout the network. We then design a hardware-efficient full-event architecture, named ExSpike, which supports the optimized pure event-driven dataflow and an additional Attention Core for spike-driven self-attention. To further improve computing efficiency, we introduce adjacent-position event compression to reduce redundant accumulations across spatially adjacent spike sequences. ExSpike is implemented on an AMD Xilinx Virtex-7 FPGA and evaluated on both classification and segmentation workloads. Experimental results show that ExSpike achieves high normalized energy efficiency across diverse SNN models while maintaining competitive accuracy, delivering up to 479.15 GOPS, 281.85 GOPS/W, and 0.80 GOPS/W/PE. In particular, ExSpike achieves up to 10$\times$ higher PE-normalized energy efficiency than the SOTA FPGA-based SNN accelerator (FireFly-T). The code for ExSpike is available at \url{https://github.com/xiaoyuehai/ExSpike}.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 传统计算模型无法刻画区块链共识的本质，本研究提出利用拓扑理论（层论）作为描述去中心化系统共识生成与全局真值构建的数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如有限状态机FSM）无法有效处理区块链的核心——分布式环境下的共识机制，将其视为“复杂的实现细节”，未能反映共识过程作为计算本质的地位。

Method: 通过范畴论（Category Theory）特别是层论（Sheaf Theory）的视角，将区块链的分布式节点与共识机制建模为局部一致性向全局真值演进的系统。

Result: 论证了传统计算模型在区块链建模上的局限性，并提出了利用拓扑理论构建区块链系统数学模型的新研究范式。

Conclusion: 拓扑理论（范畴论中层论的理论）是描述区块链分布式共识与局部一致性构建全局真值系统的天然数学语言，能够弥补有限状态机等传统计算模型无法刻画共识本质的缺陷。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [277] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对传统网络模型难以描述复杂分布式系统的问题，本文提出引入层理论，利用层拉普拉斯算子实现复杂数据的高效融合与网络共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于简单图（simple graph）的网络模型在描述现代分布式系统时存在局限性，无法充分刻画系统中异质代理、多维多模态数据流及复杂的上下文相关关系。

Method: 引入层理论（Sheaf Theory）框架，并提出利用层拉普拉斯算子（Sheaf Laplacian）作为数据融合和分布式传感网络达成共识的机制。

Result: 证明了层理论能够有效捕捉复杂系统的多层次特征，并验证了层拉普拉斯算子在解决分布式网络中的数据融合与共识问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: 层理论（Sheaf Theory）为现代复杂分布式系统的建模和分析提供了一种比传统简单图模型更强大、更具表达力的数学语言。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [278] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对稀疏注意力模型在长文本推理中因 KV Cache 传输导致的瓶颈，本文提出了基于 CXL 的 SAC 系统，通过按需获取 Top-k KV 条目，显著提升了推理吞吐量并降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）长上下文推理的瓶颈已从计算转向内存。传统的基于 RDMA 的存算分离方案在应对稀疏注意力模型时，由于强制粗粒度地搬运整个 KV Cache，导致了严重的带宽浪费和本地内存占用，无法发挥稀疏模型的能效。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SAC 的高效存算分离 KV Cache 系统。该系统利用 CXL（Compute Express Link）的低延迟、缓存行粒度（cache-line granularity）的访存语义，实现仅在推理时按需获取 Top-k 最活跃 KV 条目的机制。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 SGLang 框架下的评估显示：相比 RDMA 基准，SAC 吞吐量提升 2.1 倍，首字延迟（TTFT）降低 9.7 倍，Token 间延迟（TBT）降低 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: CXL 是实现稀疏注意力模型（Sparse Attention Models）存算分离（Disaggregated Memory）的优选基础设施，能显著优于传统的 RDMA 方法。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [279] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为非互质EJ网络提供了一种基于局部切换演算的、公式化的最小切换哈密尔顿圈分解方法，并通过符号化证明取代了传统的枚举式证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非互质（$d>1$）的Eisenstein--Jacobi（EJ）网络，现有的分解方法依赖于矩形表示和复杂的交换调度，本文旨在探索一种更简洁、基于公式的局部切换构造方法。

Method: 在自然的Cayley几何中开发了一种局部切换演算（local switch calculus）。通过使用最小可能的 $d-1$ 次组件间切换来构建前两个哈密尔顿圈，并将第三个因子作为剩余边的补集。证明过程分解为组件标签坍缩、锚点抵消、提升切换代表元的非碰撞分析以及连通补集关联四个关键要素。

Result: 为欧几里得剩余族提供了一种紧凑的、基于公式的最小切换分解，并对特定参数范围内的补集关联性进行了符号化证明。明确区分了计算验证（作为复现工具）与理论证明（作为逻辑支撑），确保了证明的严谨性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种用于非互质（non-coprime）Eisenstein-Jacobi（EJ）网络中边不相交哈密尔顿圈（EDHC）分解的公式化、最小切换构造方法，通过代数证书证明了其补集关联性，避免了依赖有限枚举的证明方式。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [280] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向循环网络在两跳范围内的故障容错设计，通过优化生成器配置，解决了如何高效实现 $f$-中继故障容错的问题，并证明了相比传统方法，优化后的设计在冗余度和容错表现上具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有向循环网络（circulant networks）在故障容错设计中的关键问题：在给定 $n$ 个节点和度数预算 $m$ 的约束下，如何最大化共享中继（shared-relay）的多重性以确保网络具备 $f$-中继故障容错能力。

Method: 1. 建立基于循环差分多重性条件的故障容错两跳原语框架；2. 定义度数-冗余景观参数 $R(n, m)$ 和 $D_f(n)$；3. 实施中继表预处理与查找算法；4. 对526,539个生成器集合进行大规模实验基准测试。

Result: 提出了一套网络设计框架；证明了传统区间生成器的局限性；通过优化设计实现了极高的容错效率，并在小规模网络中进行了精确校准，通过实验验证了设计方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 生成器集合的选择对循环网络在两跳故障容错能力（中继冗余）方面至关重要。优化的阈值设计方案能使冗余接近最优理论界限（1.16–1.63倍），而传统的区间生成器在即使具有较大度数时也可能存在结构性的故障容错缺陷。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [281] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对稠密 EJ 网络，本文提出了一种名为 EJ-MOEM 的高效广播修复算法，通过重新连接修剪后的故障树来构建新的广播生成树，并从理论和实验上证明了其在低修复深度和最小化修复边开销方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决稠密 Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) 网络在面对故障节点时，如何实现高效、非冗余的广播数据修复问题，以维持一对多通信的可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EJ-MOEM 的多方向边最小修复方法。该方法评估一系列六边形广播树方向，选择感知故障的候选方案，将修剪后的故障树收缩为健康组件，并通过外部连接边重新连接这些组件，形成一棵新的健康生成树。

Result: 提出了 EJ-MOEM 算法；证明了连接健康组件所需的修复边数量为 c-1 的必要充分性；证明了该算法在单故障和双故障场景下的最大深度边界定理；通过从 t=2 到 t=200 的广泛模拟验证了算法的 100% 成功率。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM 是一种在稠密 EJ 网络中进行非冗余一对多广播修复的有效方法。研究证明了该方法在单故障情况下修复深度最多为 t+1，双故障情况下最多为 t+2，且所需修复边数量为 c-1（c 为健康分量数）。大量仿真验证了该理论的正确性。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [282] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究通过对2025年以太坊及Base链的实证分析，揭示了当前EVM气费计量模型因忽视资源消耗多样性和状态高度依赖性而变得不再适用，并建议建立多维气费计价体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVM气费机制假设执行环境稳定且资源成本固定。论文旨在评估该假设在当前L1/L2环境下的偏离程度，即验证资源组合的变化及状态漂移（State Drift）对交易执行和气费的影响。

Method: 对2025年以太坊（L1）和Base（L2）的交易进行细粒度追踪分析，采用每日抽样3,000个区块的规模。通过将交易拆解为操作码级气费、固有气费、退款和持久状态增量，并利用旧状态下的重执行（Re-execution）测试，量化资源占用比例及状态敏感度。

Result: 1.资源差异显著：Base以读和计算为主，以太坊以状态写为主；Base更易产生持久状态增长（456GB vs 38GB）。 2.执行结果不稳定：46%的Base交易和13.9%的以太坊交易在不同历史状态下气费预估差异巨大。 3.状态敏感性高：状态重叠度低使得访问列表（Access Lists）失效，增加并行执行难度。

Conclusion: 当前EVM基于单一标量气费计费模式的假设已失效。研究证实资源消耗分布显著不均，且执行结果对状态高度敏感，这严重影响了交易预测的准确性。因此，该研究呼吁向多维气费计量（Multi-dimensional gas metering）转型，并针对持久状态增长进行明确的价格定价。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [283] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对多模态 LLM 训练中因样本长度不可预知导致的 GPU 利用率低问题，ODB 通过将批次构建移至预处理之后，实现了在线动态批处理，在不影响模型质量的前提下大幅提升了训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代复杂 LLM 训练流程（如多模态扩展、增强）导致样本在进入批处理前真实长度不可知，传统的离线批处理采样器因无法预知填充、内存占用和 GPU 饱和度，导致 GPU 利用率低下。

Method: 提出“在线动态批处理”（Online Dynamic Batching, ODB），将批次构建环节从预处理前移至预处理、增强、分词及多模态扩展之后，使其能准确感知样本真实长度。同时形式化定义了“分布式组对齐问题”（DGAP），并设计了确保无死锁、有界终止的同步机制。

Result: 在多个主流数据集和规模（2B/8B）上，相比标准固定批次方法，ODB 实现了 1.58-4.43 倍的吞吐量提升，同时在与离线预算神谕（GMT/BMT）的对比中表现优异或持平，且保持了同等的训练质量。

Conclusion: ODB 是一个适用于异构 LLM 微调场景的在线动态批处理系统，在无需预计算长度缓存或修改内核的前提下，实现了高吞吐量、训练质量可比性及正式的分布式对齐保证。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [284] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一款针对超大规模 GPU 集群的低开销（<2%）、全时段细粒度性能诊断系统，通过层级观测与高效数据压缩，实现了自动化的生产环境故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模 LLM 训练中，传统的资源监控难以定位根因，而细粒度的分析工具存在高开销（5%-30%）和数据量巨大的问题，导致无法在生产环境中进行 7x24 小时的常态化部署。

Method: 提出一种名为 ARGUS 的系统，通过以下手段实现目标：1. 多层级观测（CPU 调用栈、框架语义、GPU 内核执行）；2. 高效数据压缩（将原始内核事件压缩约 3700 倍）；3. 渐进式诊断框架（从迭代级、阶段级到内核级自动隔离故障窗口、滞后节点和受损内核）。

Result: ARGUS 在 10,000+ GPU 集群上运行超过 6 个月，额外开销低于 2%，单步数据量从 10MB 降低至 2.7KB；成功检测并优化了多种隐蔽异常，包括算力滞后、链路降级、流水线气泡放大及 FlashAttention JIT 卡顿等。

Conclusion: ARGUS 证明了在大规模 GPU 集群（10,000+）中实现低开销、全时段的细粒度可观测性是可行的，能够有效支持持续的性能监控与故障诊断，在实际生产环境中克服了传统监控工具在开销与数据规模间的权衡难题。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [285] [The Representational Limit of Scalar Interactions: An Interventional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19410)
*Potito Aghilar,Sabino Roccotelli,Stanislao Fidanza,Vito Walter Anelli,Sebastiano Stramaglia,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出 Stochastic Hi-Fi 方法，通过干预遮蔽推理有效解耦特征交互中的唯一性、冗余性和协同性，克服了传统指标对交互作用的解析缺陷，在多项实验中表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号对交互得分（signed pairwise interaction scores）由于本质上混淆了特征间的唯一性（Uniqueness）、冗余性（Redundancy）和协同性（Synergy），导致无法准确反映多变量交互的真实结构。

Method: 提出一种无需重训练的后验可预测性分解方法 Stochastic Hi-Fi，基于干预遮蔽推理（interventional masked inference）估算 U/R/S 配置；采用耦合菱形采样（coupled diamond sampling）实现方差缩减，并具备有限样本蒙特卡洛边界与收敛性保证。

Result: 在表格结构化因果模型（SCM）中，交互幅度恢复率比现有基线高出 411 倍；在 GPT-2 中成功分离了冗余与协同路径；在 NIH ChestX-ray14 数据集上显著提升了删除曲线下面积（Deletion AUC）。

Conclusion: Stochastic Hi-Fi 能够准确量化特征间的唯一性、冗余性和协同性，在结构化因果模型中表现优于现有的标量交互指标，并成功应用于大模型电路分析与医疗图像解释。

Abstract: Signed pairwise interaction scores fundamentally conflate uniqueness (U), redundancy (R), and synergy (S). We prove this on a minimal 3-way XOR structural causal model: faithful indices such as Shapley-Taylor return zero per pair, whereas projective indices such as Shapley Interaction spread the third-order effect into pair scalars that conflate the three mechanisms. We introduce Stochastic Hi-Fi, a post-hoc, retraining-free predictability decomposition that estimates per-feature U/R/S profiles by interventional masked inference. The estimator provides exact interventional semantics, finite-sample Monte Carlo bounds, strict variance reduction from coupled diamond sampling, and uniform finite-vocabulary convergence. Across tabular SCMs, Stochastic Hi-Fi recovers structure missed by scalar baselines (up to 411x larger interaction-magnitude recovery ratios). It also separates redundant and synergistic heads in the GPT-2 IOI circuit. On NIH ChestX-ray14, Stochastic Hi-Fi matches GradCAM on Pointing Game and improves substantially on Deletion AUC.

</details>


### [286] [A Solver-Free Training Method for Predict-then-Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19587)
*Beichen Wan,Mo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需在训练中调用优化求解器的决策聚焦学习方法，通过测度变换简化了梯度计算，大幅提升了预测-优化模型的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测-优化（predict-then-optimize）方法在训练时，由于决策映射呈现分段恒定性（piecewise constant），导致梯度几乎处处为零；而现有的平滑处理方法（如扰动法）在求导时每次迭代都需要求解代价高昂的优化问题，难以在大规模场景下应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于“测度变换原理”（measure transformation principle）的决策聚焦学习流水线，构建了一个在训练过程中完全不需要显式求解优化问题的代理损失函数（surrogate loss）。

Result: 该方法不仅具有良好的理论保证（如费舍尔一致性和超额风险界），而且在实验中实现了与现有最先进方法相当的决策质量，同时训练速度提升了几个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于测度变换的决策聚焦学习方法，该方法在保持算法决策质量的同时，显著降低了训练的计算成本，实现了高效的预测优化训练。

Abstract: We propose a scalable method for training prediction (machine learning) models in the predict-then-optimize paradigm, where model outputs serve as coefficients for a subsequent linear optimization task. Directly minimizing the empirical decision regret is intractable for linear programming and combinatorial optimization since the decision mapping is piecewise constant, and the gradients are zero almost everywhere. While existing methods address this by smoothing the differentiation process, they suffer from scalability issues, since a computationally expensive solver call is required for every gradient evaluation. To address this, we propose a decision-focused learning pipeline based on a measure transformation principle, which yields a new surrogate loss that is completely optimization-solver-free during training. We establish theoretical guarantees, including Fisher consistency and excess risk bounds. Empirically, our method achieves decision quality competitive with state-of-the-art methods while reducing training time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [287] [Variational Consensus Monte Carlo for Bayesian Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19643)
*Julie Fendler,Francesca L. Crowe,Tom Marshall,Sylvia Richardson,Paul D. W. Kirk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦贝叶斯混合模型框架，通过变分CMC方法在保护隐私的前提下，实现了无需数据合并的自动聚类推理，并有效解决了跨节点聚类匹配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据具有高度敏感性且存在存储共享限制，传统数据汇聚方案不可行，亟需一种能够在各计算节点独立运行且能有效进行后验分布聚合的联邦学习框架。

Method: 基于共识蒙特卡洛（CMC）方法，提出一种变分CMC扩展框架：(1)支持过拟合贝叶斯混合模型以自动推断聚类数量且无需共轭性；(2)开发跨孤岛聚类匹配算法；(3)提供针对不同联邦约束的聚合策略及其选择指南。

Result: 仿真实验证明，该框架在数据分布反映潜在聚类结构时，能比全局池化数据的标准MCMC更精准地识别小规模聚类；并在英国老年人群电子健康记录的大规模实际应用中成功识别出多种共病模式。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的联邦贝叶斯混合模型框架证明了在数据隐私保护的前提下，通过分布式推理实现可靠聚类及参数估计的可行性，尤其在处理异构数据及揭示复杂电子健康记录（EHR）模式方面表现优异。

Abstract: Motivated by the privacy, sensitivity and sharing limitations of health data, we present a comprehensive pipeline for inference of Bayesian mixture models within a federated learning setting, i.e. when data cannot be fully shared or pooled across compute nodes. We adopt a Consensus Monte Carlo (CMC) approach, in which an MCMC algorithm is run independently within each data silo to estimate local posterior distributions, which are then aggregated to approximate the posterior over the full data. The variational CMC approach of Rabinovich, Angelino and Jordan (2015) [1] frames the aggregation step as a variational inference problem, but their application to mixtures assumes the number of clusters and key mixture parameters to be known. Our main methodological contributions are: (i) an extension of variational CMC to over-fitted Bayesian mixture models that infer the number of clusters and all model parameters, without requiring conjugacy; (ii) novel cluster-matching algorithms suitable for cross-silo settings in which not every cluster appears in each local dataset; (iii) a number of inference strategies for the aggregation step, matched to different federated learning constraints; and (iv) guidelines for choosing among these in practice. A comprehensive simulation study validates the framework and allows us to compare to state-of-the-art federated learning alternatives. Notably, we show that when the composition of local datasets reflects the underlying clustering structure in the data, our approach can recover small clusters with greater accuracy than standard MCMC applied to the pooled data. We illustrate the framework on large-scale electronic health record data, identifying multi-morbidity patterns in a British geriatric population.

</details>


### [288] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement for LLM-as-a-Judge Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19714)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Weiyi He,Junxi Zhang,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 作为“评测者”存在的偏差问题，论文提出了 AURA 框架，通过引入不确定性意识的迭代人工校准机制，以极低的人工成本优化了评估的可靠性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有大规模语言模型（LLM）作为评测者所面临的偏见问题，特别是弥补当前评估流程依赖于昂贵或有限的人工标注资源，以及现有过滤机制无法稳定扩展的缺陷。

Method: 提出 AURA 框架，将对 Judge 的信任度视为一种潜在变量（latent quantity），通过迭代学习人类一致性信号、传播可靠证据，并利用不确定性采样（uncertainty-aware sampling）优先选取样本进行人工核实。
